PCW — Card

Fusion 41, 6 July 2001

6 July 2001
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Fusion 41 6 Jul 2001 Rutgers University, New Brunswick, NJ A package arrives at CEO Tyler Nelson's office The scene opens as a brown UPS Delivery truck pulls up to the backdoor of the Rutgers Athletic Center on the Campus of Rutgers University, in New Brunswick, New Jersey. The brown cargo truck comes to an abrupt halt and the driver hops out through the sliding driver’s side door, clad in brown short sleeve shirt and shorts combo favored by UPS drivers during the summer, which a 18” X 18” cardboard box in hand.The camera follows behind the driver on foot. An arena security guard allows the driver to pass through the back door of the arena without even as much as a word.The camera continues to follow the swift-footed driver as he passes through the threshold of the arena, turning right once he reaches the main area of the backstage area. Winding past set up crews and several wrestlers and PCW workers, chatting or otherwise preparing for tonight’s festivities.The driver deftly winds his way through the throng of people in the back, then slips through a doorway on the left side of the hall, with the camera following close behind. Once the camera passes through the doorway, it becomes apparent that the room beyond is serving as some kind of waiting room, as leather couches line the walls, while small, magazine covered coffee tables site in front of them. At the far end of the room is a door, with a golden nameplate labled “CEO Tyler Nelson,” while in front of the door, is a small reception desk. Seated behind the desk is none other than Nelson’s right hand man and number one lackey, Mr. Simmons. Other than Simmons, the room is completely devoid of all other living beings.Simmons is wearing a shiny silver Armani suit, and seems to be fascinated by the content of a magazine, which is called “Outlaw Biker.” In fact, Simmons is so fascinated by the interior of the magazine, he has turned the magazine to the side, so he can read the content from a different angle. Beads of sweat have formed on his brow.The now winded driver throws the package up on the counter, startling Simmons who drops the magazine down behind the counter.UPS Driver: I have a package for a “T. Nelson.” Simmons: (still a bit flustered) I am Mr. Nelson’s associate. I can sign for that…Simmons anxiously reaches for the driver’s clipboard and to sign but the driver recoils.UPS Driver: Not so fast there Renegade Biker! Not any Lorenzo Lamas off the street can sign for this. Only Mr. T. Nelson can sign for it.Simmons sighs, and picks of a phone receiver from the reception desk.Simmons looks up at the ceiling, while the driver checks his watch. Finally, Simmons snaps out of his daze.Simmons: (On the phone) Sorry to disturb you, you sir. There is a UPS jockey out here….Simmons winces as garbled shouting can be heard from the other end of the phone.Simmons: (on the phone, meekly)Yes, I know sir, but he is insisting that only you can sign for it.Simmons pauses and listens intently to the voice on the other end of the phone.Simmons: (on the phone) Ok, I’ll check…Simmons motions for the UPS driver to come closer. Simmons looks at him intently for a moment.Simmons: (on the phone) No, no fake beards detectable. He does not appear to be anyone we know.Simmons once again grows silent as he listens to the voice on the other end of the phone.Simmons: (on the phone) Ok, wait a moment, I’ll check.Simmons puts the phone down and looks at the package on the reception desk. He then, gingerly puts his ear to the package, while the UPS man sighs and rolls his eyes in the foreground.The camera gets a close up shot of Simmons as more sweat appears on his brow and his eyes wince. After a slow tense moment, Simmons pulls his ear away from the package, removes a hankerchief from the front pocket of his suit jacket, then wipes his brow.Simmons: (on the phone) No, sir, the package is not ticking.Simmons pauses then nods a bit.Simmons: (on the phone) Will do.Simmons hangs up the phone and motions to the driver to follow him. Simmons gets up from his receptionist position and moves to the door bearing the nameplate of the CEO. He then grasps the golden doorknob and ushers in the driver. The cameraman follows.The scene shifts inside the comfortably furnished office of PCW CEO Tyler Nelson. The camera pans around the room, showing comfortable leather couches and walls adorned with pictures of various PCW superstars cover the walls. The camera pans left to right across the room, pausing for a moment on a huge fish tank along the rear wall.The camera pans in on the 500-gallon fish tank to reveal it that is occupied by a dozen or so massive red bellied piranhas, who are aggressively patrolling several rock formations that have been placed along the bottom of the tank, in search of food. Panning left across the tank, the camera catches a glimpse of something shiny. Panning out a bit, it becomes apparent, some sort of partially rusted title belt of some kind has been placed in the bottom of the tank. The camera focuses in on the belt, in attempt to make out any writing that may be on its metallic face. The first thing that stands out about the belt is an emblem of the US flag, which the camera focuses in on. The camera slowly below the flag, where the rapidly oxidizing words “Just Electrifying Wrestling” can be made out on the belt. A piranha swims by, the belt, and ejects a small bit or brown matter from it’s rear fin region. The camera follows the slow descent of the brown matter as it falls perfectly in to the cradle part of the “J” in JeW.The scene freezes at that point for a moment, before abruptly shifting to where the CEO of PCW, Tyler Nelson is sitting behind a huge wooden desk, scratching the ears of what appears to be a some kind of Siamese breed cat.Nelson looks up to see the UPS man and Simmons standing before him. He releases the cat from his lap.Nelson: Go ahead Frisco, eat the caviar I left for you in your bowel. Be a good pussy and run along. “Frisco” hisses at the UPS driver, then runs off.Nelson: Well, my brown uniformed, working class, hourly wage friend, let me sign my name, so you can not even think of attempting to charge UPS overtime today.The UPS man rolls his eyes and hands his clipboard to Nelson, who signs it. The UPS man holds his hand out to receive the clipboard from Nelson, who reaches out to give it to him, but drops just short of the worker’s hand, causing it to fall onto the floor.The driver scowls, bends down and picks up the clipboard, much to the amusement of both Nelson and Simmons. The driver picks up the clipboard and rushes out of the room grumbling to himself.Simmons looks at Nelson then looks anxiously at the box.Nelson: Are you sure it wasn’t ticking?Simmons: 100% sure. My hearing is perfect.Nelson: Good. Then do me a favor and open it….over there in the corner if you will, putting your body between me and the package.Nelson smiles cruelly as Simmons stares blankly at the package. Nelson: Well, chop chop! Time is money, you know.Simmons walks over to Nelson’s desk and gently picks up the package. He looks to Nelson for mercy, who in kind, merely smiles back. Simmons swallows hard and retreats to the corner with the box.The camera shifts to the vantage point of which Nelson would be watching things unfold, meaning we have a shot of Simmons’s back, as he slowly opens the package. Apparently open, Simmons pauses for a moment apparently in shock.The camera shifts back to show a front view of Nelson, who appears ready to dive beneath his desk, in case of trouble.The angle shifts back to Nelson’s vantage point as Simmons turns around and pulls a title belt out of the box.Nelson raises a curious eye brow as Nelson approaches the CEO’s desk, with the belt in one hand, and the now open package in another.Simmons places the belt on Nelson’s desk. Nelson looks down upon it and frowns.Nelson: (miffed) Bah! What the hell is this!Simmons: It appears to be one of the two JeW tag team title belts.Nelson: (rolling his eyes) I can see that, you numbskull! Simmons hangs his head in shame.Nelson: (continuing) Why is it cluttering my desk? Simmons: Shall I take it away sir? Nelson: Find some use for it. I think the maid told me Frisco’s litter scoop broke yesterday. The camera quickly shifts to a close up “Frisco”, who hisses, and lunges at the camera. The scene then shifts back to Nelson.Nelson: (continuing) Send it to our technicians and have them drill holes in it, then use it as a replacement for the old scoop.Simmons: (smiling) Very good, sir.Nelson, begins to read some paperwork. Simmons continues smiling at him. Nelson, suddenly looks up.Nelson: Well, don’t sit there looking like that goofball Jeremy Howard. Simmons: (still smiling) There’s more, sir.Nelson shrugs as if to say “get on with it man.”Simmons reaches into the box and pulls out yet another title belt and throws it on the desk. The camera focuses in on the belt which has an a huge globe emblazoned upon it, with the words “Just Electrifying Wrestling” wrapped around the top, and the words “World Champion” around the bottom.A huge grin crosses Nelson’s face.Nelson: (rubbing his hands together) Excellent!Simmons: There is a note to go with these, too.Simmons pulls a piece of yellow paper from the box, and Nelson greedily snaps it up and begins to read.Nelson: HA! That’s all he wants? A bit of air time on Sunday, in return for this? Done deal! “Frisco” hops up on to the desk and begins sniffing and scratching at the JeW tag team title belt, as the camera begins to pan out. Nelson, smiles and laughs, while holding up the JeW World Title belt…..as the scene fades to black.The usual opening for Friday Fusion showing highlights of PCW wrestlers in action upon a green lightning and explosion filled backdrop begins to roll. Wrestlers highlighted are “The Messiah” Chris Chase, Revenant, Brandi, Shawn Prodigy and Nikita Morozov.The scene fades inside of the RAC at Rutgers University as pyros shoot off from the ringposts, ceiling and entryway, and “Let Forever Be” from the Chemical Brothers hits the PA system.Cameras zoom around the arena, taking samples of the crowd. The first stop for our cameras is on a group of well endowed, tri-sigma sorority girls who are holding up a sign which reads “Miyagawa gave us free tickets, but still ain’t getting no play!”Moving away on the shameful proof of the levels Miyagawa will stoop to in attempt to get a piece of ass, the camera moves along a group of Asian youths who have painted their faces red. One member of the group is waving a huge Japanese flag, while the others in the group blow bizarre sounding horns and bang upon drums.Moving away from elegant proof of why Japan is mired in a five-year long recession, to ringside where former PIC World Heavyweight Champion Nate Dumas is shown signing autographs for fans at ringside.Finally the action moves to the ringside position of the not critically acclaimed PCW announce team.Dave Kern: Hello everyone and welcome to Primetime Championship Wrestling Friday Fusion. I am Dave Kern and I am joined here at my ringside announce position as always by my partners in crime, Jeff Marx and Steven Smith. We are coming to you live from the packed to the rafters Rutgers Athletic Center from the campus of Rutgers University, in the antiquated and run down college town of New Brunswick, New Jersey and what a night of action we have planned for you here tonight, with eight first round world title tourney matches PLUS a six man Stairway to Hell match serving as our main event.Jeff: (sarcastically) How great is that gonna be. I mean, any main event with Revenant in it HAS to be good.Dave: Revenant has held two PCW title belts during his PCW career. Steven: And he is a former UFW US Champion….Jeff: Which is a perfect example of why UFW is no more.Dave: (sighing) Well, we have an absolutely jam packed night of action for you on tap, so we are going to send you right up to the ring for our first match of the evening.“Sandstorm” by Darude hits over the PA as Ming makes his way down to the ring.Clay Clayborne: Introducing first…from Beijing, China and weighing in at 215 pounds…MING!!“One Step Closer” by Linkin Park blasts through the arena as Miyagawa makes his way out.Clay Clayborne: And his opponent…from Los Angeles, California and weighing 212 pounds…MIYAGAWA!!Miya bolts down the ramp and slides in the ring. He and Ming immediately start to trade rapid fire punches in the center of the ring as the bell sounds.Dave: This one getting underway quickly.Jeff: Hopefully Ming takes Miyajobber out just as fast.Steven: Where’s Brian Hanson when you need himThe three announcers all share a laugh.Ming and Miya fall to the mat and roll around in an all out brawl, trading right hand blows the whole time. The referee simply stands by and lets the two men go at it. Dave: I wonder if we are going to see any wrestling moves in this one at all!Jeff: Neither of them actually know any, so probably not.The buzzsaw that is Miyagawa and Ming rolls out to the arena floor and they continue to battle. Ming gains the upper hand with a quick rake of the eyes, then follows up by sending Miya in to the steel ring steps. Miya crashes into the unforgiving steps with a sickening thud and sends them flying.Crowd: Ooooooh!Miya clutches his back as Ming moves in on the attack. Miya springs up off the ground and spears Ming to the arena floor. Miya then mounts Ming and starts to lay into him with a barrage of right and left hand shots. Miya jumps up off of the stunned Ming and hops up on the apron. He climbs into the ring and runs the ropes, coming off the opposite side and then flying up and over nailing Ming with a SENTON BOMB OVER THE TOP ROPE TO THE FLOOR!Dave: OH MY GOD!!!!The crowd explodes.The Crowd: PCW!!! PCW!!! PCW!!! PCW!!!Dave: A SENSATIONAL MOVE BY MIYAGAWA!Jeff: I wasn’t that impressed.Miya shakes his head to clear the cobwebs and then drags Ming to his feet, shooting him into the ring under the bottom rope. Miya then reaches under the ring and pulls out a table.Crowd: WOOD!! WOOD!! WOOD!! WOOD!!Miya sets the table up on the outside and then rolls into the ring where he is met by the boot of Ming.Dave: Miya took too much time getting that table out and that allowed Ming to recover from that Senton Bomb.Jeff: That just shows how stupid Miyajobber is.Ming lands a couple of more boots to Miya’s head and stomach before pulling him to his feet and getting him in a front face lock. Ming hoists Miya up in a suplex position, holding him in the air for 1…2…3…4…5 seconds before bringing him crashing down to the mat with a BRAINBUSTER!Jeff: YEAH! BREAK THAT JOBBER’S NECK!! Dave: (disgusted) You are just sick.Jeff: Thanks!Ming hops to his feet like a cat and then bounds to the top turnbuckle. He pauses momentarily before coming off with an ASAI MOONSAULT! Ming makes a cover …1…2…Miya is able to get a shoulder up. Ming rolls Miya onto his stomach and locks him up in an STF!Dave: He had Miya hooked right in the center of the ring!Jeff: Give it up, Miyajobber! Remember Nagasaki and the deformities that went along with it!Dave: (sighing) I apologize for my colleague Jeff Marx if any of you fans watching at home were offended by that comment.Jeff: Oh yeah, how about this one. Okinawa is full of hookers, and it ain’t rape if you pay for it!Dave sighs.Miya grimaces as Ming wrenches back on the hold. With Miya’s face exposed Ming lands a few punches to Miya’s mouth, splitting his lip open.Jeff: BLEED JOBBER, BLEED!!Dave: My God, Jeff! Will you get a hold of yourself?Steven: Yeah, Jeff, grab yourself and be more like me!The referee checks with Miya who shakes his head ‘NO’ emphatically. Miya tastes the blood coming from his mouth and seems to become incensed, his eyes glazing over narrowing into a glare. Miya starts to claw his way towards the ropes as Ming does all he can to halt the progress. Finally Miya reaches the bottom rope and forces the break in the hold. Dave: What a show of heart there by Miyagawa!Ming gets to his feet and pulls Miya up by his hair. Ming sends Miya into the ropes, but Miya hooks the top rope to stop his momentum. Ming wildly charges in and tries to clothesline Miya over the top rope, but Miya ducks and back body drops Ming up and over the top. Ming is able to grab the rope, however, and lands feet first on the apron.Dave: They are fighting right above that table Miyagawa set up earlier!Miya turns quickly around only to be caught with a right hand from Ming. Ming goes for another punch, but Miya ducks this one and fires a shoulder into the gut of Ming. Thinking fast Miya grabs the top rope and propels himself up and over, rolling over Ming and in the process grabbing Mings head and taking him off the apron for a NECKBREAKER OFF THE APRON THROUGH THE TABLE TO THE FLOOR!!Dave: OH MY GOD!!!!The crowd explodes.The Crowd: PCW!! PCW!! PCW!! PCW!! PCW!!! PCW!!!Dave: OH MY GOD!!Jeff: Awww Ca-hrist!Both men are lying in the remains of the shattered table. Miya is the first to start moving and he crawls over to Ming and then pulls him up, rolling him into the ring. Miya claws his way into the ring as well and stands against the ropes. Ming starts to try and stand himself and Miya measures him up. As soon as Ming gets to his feet Miya springs on his back and LOCKS IN THERAPY!!Dave: MIYAGAWA HAS THERAPY LOCKED IN ON MING RIGHT IN THE CENTER OF THE RING!!Jeff: Fight comrade!! Fight!!Dave: Speaking of “comrades” where the hell is the Red Army and their token run in on this match?Ming goes down as Miya wrenches in the hold tighter. Suddenly the Soviet Flag appears on the jumbotron and the arena is set in a red glow. From the back comes the Red Army.Jeff: You were saying?Dave: NO!!!!Jeff: (springing to his feet and saluting) My Comrades have finally arrived to stop this joke of a match!Ivan Stanislav, Tom Walczak, Nikita Morozov, Alexi Ruslan and Referee Speedy Riggs stalk toward the ring as the referee checks on Ming. He is holding on valiantly as Miya continues to apply the pressure with Therapy.Jeff: HOLD ON COMRADE MING! HELP IS ON THE WAY!!The Red Army charges the ring and slides inside.Dave: NOOOOOOO!!!Morozov rushes referee Bob Charlie, kicks him in the gut, then crushes him with a DDT.The crowd explodes into a chorus of boos.Dave: (outraged) This is a travesty!Miyagawa gets up and backs into a corner, but finds himself surrounded. Meanwhile, Alexei Ruslan helps Ming to his feet.Jeff: KILL!!! KILL!! KILL!!! BURY HIM!!Miyagawa begs off from the approaching Stanislav.Jeff: Look at him beg. What a sissy!Ming is up on his feet. Miyagawa suddenly nods, and Ming springs forth, nailing Ruslan with an elbow to the jaw.Jeff: WHAAAAAAT?!?!?!?!The crowd explodes.Dave: What is going on?!?!?Stanislav to see what the commotion is, just to catch a kick to his Siberian region from Miyagawa.Ivan: (screaming) NNNNNNNYYYYYYYYYEEEEEEETTTTTT!!!!Ivan doubles over in pain. Miyagawa grabs Ivan by the head, and nails the seven footer with a face buster. The crowd explodes.Dave: THIS WAS ALL A SET UP!!! MING AND MIYAGAWA ARE WORKING TOGETHER!!!Mirozov and Walczak charge Miyagawa.Steven: LOOK!!!!!Suddenly, the camera switches to ringside, where Kensuke Suzahara and Inphino Blitz hope the guard rail and into the ring.Dave: Suzahara and Blitz are here!!!! They have ambushed the Red Army!!!Jeff: (apathetically) Great.Kensuke and Inphino head off the Red Army wielding the kendo sticks as swords. Meanwhile, in the ring, Ming lays down and Miyagawa makes the cover.Jeff: What the hell is this?!?!Dave: I think Ming is giving Miyagawa the win, because Miya had the match all but won before the Red Army came out!Referee Speedy Riggs doesn’t want to count the pinfall, but the original referee Bob Charlie has regained his senses and makes the count…..1……2……3!!!Dave: Miyagawa wins this bizarre match, but the situation is still breaking down.Jeff: KILL THE JAPS, COMRADES!!The showdown continues as Miyagawa and Ming join Kensuke and Inphino in the standoff with the Red Army. Neither side gives so much as an inch as they stare coldly at each other. Suddenly a swarm of officials rush out from the back to separate the two parties. The crowd erupts into a chorus of boos.Dave: These two factions are definitely heading for a showdown in the near future.Jeff: It will be swift and deadly. The Asians will be destroyed.Dave: Well, there are many who would not agree with that assessment. We need to head to a commercial. We will be right back.(Commercial: PCW New Dawning, Sunday, August 19th, 2001, Live and Only on Pay Per View. Call your local cable company or satellite provider today to order!)The show opens back up in the back stage area as a still wincing Ivan Stanislav, bloody mouthed Alexei Ruslan, Tom Walczak, Nikita Morozov and Loyal Communist Party Member Wang are gathered in the Red Army’s lockerroom, Ivan: (screaming) Damn Ming!!! I will melt the flesh from his yellow hide!!!Alexei: Nevermind him now. The Chinese were always our pawns in the past anyway, as they were always the weak link in the Communist chain.The camera shifts to a blankly blinking Communist Party Member Wang.Alexei: Except for you, Wang, of course.Wang smiles and nods dumbly in agreement.Alexei: Let the yellow ones stick together. We have bigger fish to catch. Now, remember the plan…..All members of the group huddle in close, while Alexei begins to whisper his instructions, as the camera pans out.The scene suddenly shifts out of the Red Army locker room to a shot where Jay and AJ Storm are talking while at the catering table in the backstage area...Jay Storm: Man, you should know that Twisted Metal: Black, is BY FAR, the best video game for the PS2! AJ Storm: You're wrong, Jay. Smuggler's Run beats Twisted Metal by a mile! Jay Storm: Yeah, but in Twisted Metal, you get to blow up stuff! AJ Storm: You got a point there, bro... Just then, PCW's Only Female Wrestler, Brandi, walks into the scene.Jay Storm: Listen Brandi, I'm sorry about what happened...to John and all... Brandi: That's not what's on my mind, Jay... AJ Storm: What happened? Brandi: Some lunatic broke into my hotel room and wrote "I LOVE YOU, BRANDI" all over my walls. And not only that, the hotel made me pay for the damages! Jay Storm: Damn, that sucks. You know, if you would let me sleep in your ….Brandi: (interrupting) Don’t even finish that sentence. Suddenly, Jeremy Howard walks by.Jeremy Howard: Hey Brandi. Brandi nods, as Howard walks off. She has a slight frown on her face, as if new evidence has been brought up...Brandi: Hmmm….he’s never talked to me before. Brandi walks off. The scene fades with the Storm brothers having quizzical looks on their faces...The scene shifts back to the ringside position of our announce team.Dave: Someone ransacked Brandi’s hotel room?Jeff: She probably got drunk and invited a football team back to her room for an gang bang last night and is trying to cover it all up.Dave: Oh come on, don’t besmirch Brandi’s reputation like that.Jeff: Dave, you were at the Storm family BBQ, right?Dave: Yes, and?Jeff: Brandi was drunk and slutting it up there with anyone who wanted a piece.Dave: Oh, come on!Jeff: It’s true, I swear! She came on to me and begged for it, but I turned her down.Dave: Yeah, and Ethiopians turn down lobster dinners and steaks too. Just shut the hell up so we can send it up to Clay in the ring.Dave: We have two of PCW2's brightest stars ready to compete for a chance at the second round. Jeff: Who cares! PCW2 is DEAD! Steve: Kill! Kill! Kill! Dave: You two dorks, shut up! Clay, take it over man. "Forever" by Forth Effect hits over the PA as Ian Preston makes his way to the ring. Clay: First, coming down the ring aisle, weighing in at 237 pounds, from Milwaukee, Wisconsin........ IAN PRESTON!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! Jeff: Jobber! Jobber! Steve: Knob job. Knob job! SMACK! Jeff: You almost hit me, watch out next time Dave. Dave: Sorry, next time move out of the way. "Anima" by Tool hits over the PA as Deadly Dustin Downey makes his way to the ring. Clay: And his opponent, coming to the ring area, weighing at 229 from Dallas Texas............ DEADLY DUSTIN DOWNEY!!!!!!!!!!!!Downey hits the ring fast. Preston goes to lay the boots on him, but Downey rolls out of the way and grabs Preston's leg. Downey hesitates for a second, then he leg whips him to the mat. DD gets to his feet and bounces off the ropes. He gets close and nails Preston with an elbow to the head. Preston reaches for his head in pain. Jeff: Hopefully that knocked some sense into that whiny crybaby. Steve: I got something that will knock some sense in him. Dave: SHUT UP! DD reaches down and grabs Preston. He throws Preston into the ropes. IP bounces off into the foot of DD. Preston starts to fall back, but DD helps him with a vicious clothesline. DD gets back to his feet and runs the ropes again. Moments later, he hits IP with a knee to the stomach. IP is reeling. Dave: Ian Preston better pick up the offense, of Downey is moving onto the second round fast! Jeff: He has no offense that can stop Downey. Downey goes to lift Preston up to his feet, but on the way up, Preston delivers a low blow to Downey. Jeff: I stand corrected, the nut shot always seems to work. Preston delivers an elbow to the back of Downey's head. Downey falls forward. Preston bounces off the ropes, then delivers a low dropkick to the side of Downey's abdomen. This, in turn, sends Downey to the outside. Preston goes up to the top rope. Jeff: Great, what is this loser going to do now? Kill the both? Dave: Just wait and see. Jeff: Why? I know what's going to happen. Downey groggily gets back to his feet. Preston flies off the top rope and grabs Downey with his legs into a huricanrana. Downey goes flying clear across the side of the ring. Preston walks over to Downey and picks him up. IP goes to send DD into the steps, but DD counters with a short clothesline out of desperation. Both men are down. Jeff: I told you he'd screw this match up. He's killed them both. Dave: They're not dead.......... yet. Steve: No! I got plans for.......... SMACK! Preston gets back to his feet, as DD is up to a knee. Preston grabs DD and throws him back into the ring, as Preston follows. Downey stands winded in the corner. Preston goes for the spear into the corner, but he misses when DD steps out of the way. Prestons shoulder hits the steel post. Preston starts to walk backwards in pain, grabbing his shoulder. DD waits for him, and waits for him, then he hits the German suplex on him. DD wastes no time going back to work as he makes the ascent up the top rope. Jeff: Haven't these two learned yet that the top rope is not there friend?Dave: I guess not Jeff. Maybe you should get in there and tell them. Jeff: No way, I'll leave that job for Steve. Steve: Bend them over. Dave: You two sickos can shut up now. DD stands on the top rope and waits for IP to stand up. It takes a few minutes, but when he does, IP gets hit with the flying forearm to the forehead. DD grabs IP up and stuffs his head between his legs. Steve: Woohoo! This is my favorite part. SMACK! Steve: But I just want to see...... SMACK! Jeff: That’s three this match alone. DD lifts IP up in the air and brings him crashing down with the powerbomb. DD goes down to make the cover. The ref starts. 1... 2... KICK OUT! DD sits up in amazement. Jeff: H isn't going to by a win like that. Better luck next time, loser! Dave: Dustin Downey almost had this match in the bag with that powerbomb. Jeff: But he didn't! DD grabs Preston and goes for the cradle piledriver, but Preston counters it by shoving him off into the ropes. DD bounces off the ropes and into a spear by Preston. DD hits the mat hard. Preston lifts up DD over his shoulder and hits the shoulder breaker. Then he goes up to the top rope. Preston gloats for a moment up there, before he launches himself off with a huge frog splash. IP goes for the cover..... 1..... 2..... KICK OUT! Preston is furious. Jeff: Neither of these jokes can win. Please make this match end soon. Dave: Just call the match, someone will win. Steve: I'll go in there and spank them. Jeff: I think those two fruitcakes would actually like it. Steve goes to get up from his seat, but Dave stops him in mid-stride. Dave: Don't even think of it. Call the match. Ian Preston launches DD into the corner of the turnbuckle. DD hits hard, but stays there. Preston goes for the big splash in the corner, but DD moves out of the way. DD catches Preston close to the turnbuckle and uses it to his advantage for a tornado DDT. Preston is planted. Dave: DOWNEY PLANTED PRESTON WITH THE TORNADO DDT!!!!!!! DD goes up the top rope, with his back turned to his opponent. Moments later he comes flying off with the Deadly Reckonings! Dave: DEADLY RECKONINGS! DEADLY RECKONINGS! DD makes the cover on Preston's lifeless body. The ref makes the count. 1... 2... 3!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!Jeff: Another jobber in the second round. Just what we needed. Dave: DUSTIN DOWNEY IS MOVING ONTO THE SECOND ROUND TO FACE EITHER SNOW OR WHITE MEXICAN!!!!!!!!!!! Jeff: Who cares? None of those three are going to win it. Preston sits up in disgust. He gets to his feet and starts to leave the ring. Suddenly the crowd pops.Dave: LOOK!!!!On his way though, he happens to run into Jeremy "Krayzie" Howard. Krayzie hits the ring and nails a standing side kick to Preston. Preston falls back on his back. Dave: KRAYZIE IS TAKING IT TO PRESTON! Steven: Why?!?Jeff: It’s Preston. That’s reason enough!!!!Dave: Well said!!!Jeremy goes to the outside of the ring and pulls back the mat on the floor, revealing the cold hard concrete below it. The crowd explodes.Dave: This can’t be good for Preston.Jeff: Anytime Preston steps into the ring, its not good for the fans, so he should share out pain a bit.Dave sighs.Howard gets back in the ring and grabs Preston. Jeremy grabs Preston and throws him into the turnbuckle. Preston lies hunched over it. Jeremy goes over to the corner, grabs Preston and lifts him up to the top rope. A videotape labelled Special Friend The scene opens as a brown UPS Delivery truck pulls up to the backdoor of the Rutgers Athletic Center on the Campus of Rutgers University, in New Brunswick, New Jersey. The brown cargo truck comes to an abrupt halt and the driver hops out through the sliding driver’s side door, clad in brown short sleeve shirt and shorts combo favored by UPS drivers during the summer, which a 18” X 18” cardboard box in hand.The camera follows behind the driver on foot. An arena security guard allows the driver to pass through the back door of the arena without even as much as a word.The camera continues to follow the swift-footed driver as he passes through the threshold of the arena, turning right once he reaches the main area of the backstage area. Winding past set up crews and several wrestlers and PCW workers, chatting or otherwise preparing for tonight’s festivities.The driver deftly winds his way through the throng of people in the back, then slips through a doorway on the left side of the hall, with the camera following close behind. Once the camera passes through the doorway, it becomes apparent that the room beyond is serving as some kind of waiting room, as leather couches line the walls, while small, magazine covered coffee tables site in front of them. Then Kanton slowly starts to ascend the ladder.Dave: KANTON IS GOING UP THE LADDER!!Jeff: Not for long.As Kanton gets about half way up the ladder, Darkstar rushes over and pushes the ladder over causing Kanton to fly off and crotch himself on the top rope.Crowd: OOOOH!!Dave: YIKES!Jeff: YOWZA!Steven: MMMMMM…!The loud meaty smack is heard followed by the usual whimpering.Kanton straddles the rope for a few seconds before Darkstar dropkicks him off to the floor. Nosferatu is up in the ring and he and Darkstar set up the ladder again. Nosferatu holds the ladder as Darkstar starts up. Naket gets to his feet and grabs a hold of Vampir. Naket takes Nosferatu up and over with a belly to back suplex. Dave: The action is fast an furious.Steven: Like my Boca after some tequilas!Revenant scampers to his feet and climbs up after Darkstar who is almost to the top of the ladder. Revenant grabs one of Darkstar’s legs and stops his progress. Darkstar tries to shake Revenant off but his grip is too tight. Suddenly Stryfe comes flying through the picture and nails Revenant with a missile dropkick. Revenant goes shooting off the ladder but unfortunately for the Dark Age he pulls Darkstar off with him and they both go crashing to the mat. Dave: Someone is going to die before this match is over.Jeff: We can only hope that it is Naket.Stryfe starts to go and check on Darkstar but decides to go up the ladder instead. Stryfe is making good progress and is halfway up when Kanton gets in the ring and starts up the ladder after him. Stryfe sees him and surprisingly decides to wait for Kanton.Jeff: WHAT ARE YOU DOING? GET UP THAT LADDER!As Kanton gets close Stryfe reaches over the ladder and grabs Kanton’s head. He rams it into the barbed wire on the ladder further busting Kanton open.Jeff: Sorry I doubted you. Good job!Stryfe slams Kanton’s head into the ladder several more times, but Kanton fights back and fires a solid right hand to the face of Stryfe. Kanton then rips a piece of barbed wire off the cage and wraps it around his hand, then starts to pound away on the head of Stryfe. Stryfe’s head is lacerated severely as Kanton continues to hit him with the barbed wire covered fist. Suddenly Darkstar nails Kanton in the back with the steel chair that was lying in the ring. Kanton clings to the ladder for dear life as Stryfe climbs and gets to the top of the ladder.Dave: Stryfe is looking to be the first one to the top of the cell.His face a full crimson mask, Stryfe balances on top of the ladder and jumps up. He grabs the ceiling of the cell and pulls himself up and through the top.Jeff: YEEEEEESSSSS!!Dave: Don’t speak too soon, Jeff. Kanton is going to make it through as well.Just as Dave said, Kanton has made his way up and through the cell. Stryfe is waiting for him and greets him with a boot to the face. Then the former PCW World Champion drags Kanton to his feet and takes him over with a BELLY TO BELLY SUPLEX ON THE TOP OF THE CAGE! Stryfe then makes his way over and begins to climb up the rope.Dave: STRYFE IS GOING TO RING THE BELL!! HE’S GOING TO WIN THIS MATCH FOR DARK AGE!Stryfe continues to progress towards the bell slowly but surely. Suddenly another head pops up from inside the cell.Dave: IT’S SCOTT NAKET! NAKET IS OUT OF THE CELL!Jeff: SOMEONE THROW HIM OFF THE SIDE!!Naket sees Stryfe scaling the rope and jumps up trying to grab a foot. Stryfe picks his feet up so Naket can’t reach them, but it causes him to stop climbing. Naket grabs the bottom of the rope and starts to swing it around, trying to throw Stryfe off balance. Stryfe clings to the rope for dear life but can’t maintain his grip and falls to the top of the cell. Naket goes over and grabs Stryfe’s head. Naket drags him over to the opening in the top of the cell and attempts to throw him back in.Dave: NAKET IS TRYING TO KILL STRYFE!Vampir Nosferatu has made his way up the ladder now. He reaches up and rakes Naket’s eyes, causing him to let go of Stryfe. Nosferatu gets up on the top of the cell and begins to lay waste to Naket. Vampir grabs him around the throat and lifts him high in the air. Naket struggles in vain as VAMPIR DRIVES NAKET DOWN ON THE TOP OF THE CAGE WITH A CHOKESLAM!!Dave: OH MY GOD!!!!The crowd explodes.The Crowd: PCW!!! PCW!!! PCW!!! PCW!!! PCW!!!Dave: UNBELEVABLE SHOW OF STRENGTH! AFTER THE BEATING THESE MEN HAVE ENDURED TO BE ABLE TO DO THAT….Nosferatu now starts to climb the rope towards the bell. Kanton also grabs onto the rope and makes his way up. He and Nosferatu exchange blows as they climb up the rope. The two men are near the top and both men reach out for the bell. Suddenly the Russian flag appears on the Jumbotron once again and the arena gets that red hue to it.Jeff: COME MY COMRADES!! KILL THEM ALL!!Dave: WHAT BUSINESS DOES THE RED ARMY HAVE IN THIS MATCH?!Jeff: Does it matter? We can do as we please and there is no one that can stop us.Stanislav, Walczak, Morozov, and Ruslan make their way down the ramp and to the cage. Ivan walks over to the door of the cell and rips it off the hinges with a mighty roar. The four men enter the cell and climb into the ring. Revenant and Darkstar are both on the ladder and look at the Red Army with stunned expressions. Ivan stalks over and with one arm pushes the ladder over sending both Revenant and Darkstar tumbling to the outside. Dave: STANISLAV HAS LAID WASTE TO REVENANT AND DARKSTAR!Back up on top Kanton and Nosferatu are struggling to reach the bell. Both men are reaching and the bell sounds!Dave: Who rung the bell??Jeff: Had to be Nosferatu. Kanton is too much of an American pig to be able to do such a simple task.Dave: SIMPLE?! THESE MEN WENT THROUGH HELL TO GET TO THAT BELL!!Jeff: I am afraid you are wrong there, Comrade Dave. Hell is about to climb up on top of the cell.As the words still roll off Jeff’s tongue we see that the Red Army has scaled the ladder and is now on top of the cage. The first one in their path is Naket. The Canadian tries to fight off the Russians but they are too much for him. Stanislav overpowers Naket and lifts him by the throat as Walczak, Morozov and Ruslan work on Stryfe. Ivan pounds on his chest with his free hand before nailing Naket with the IRON CURTAIN!Dave: THIS IS ABSOLUTE CARNAGE!Jeff: I LOVE IT!!Dave: WHO’S COMING DOWN THE RAMP NOW?!We switch over to see Jason Folts, Rich Cannon, and Seth Jackson running down the ramp and into the cell.Jeff: More Canadian trash! Comrades, destroy them!The three Canadians make their way up the ladder to the top of the cell. They head straight for Stanislav who is standing over the fallen Scott Naket. Meanwhile, Kanton and Nosferatu have come down off the rope and are battling the Red Army as well. Kanton squares off with Morozov as Nosferatu goes toe to toe with Walczak. Foltz, Cannon, and Jackson are all pounding on the big Russian bear and beat him down to one knee. They continue their barrage until a thunderous roar is heard as Ivan stands up and throws the Canadians off of him. They go flying all over the top of the cell as the Russian raises his massive arms in the air as he releases another roar that reverberates throughout the arena. Dave: Can anyone stop this Russian monster?!Jeff: Nope.Suddenly, the crowd pops.Now there is another figure making it’s way down the ramp.Dave: WHAT IN THE HELL IS GOING ON HERE?!Jeff: This stupid USA Kid wants some punishment from the Red Army as well it would seem.The USA Kid comes down and climbs up the side of the cell all the way to the top. He goes over to where Kanton is being stomped by Morozov and Ruslan. USA Kid runs and dives at the two Russians, nailing them with a double clothesline.Dave: Look at the top of that cell buckling, Jeff! I’m not sure how much longer it will be able to hold. There are twelve PCW superstars on top of the cell with two more in the ring underneath.Jeff: It’s definitely going to buckle now….The crowd pops again.Steven: LOOK!Down the ramp and making his way to the ring is……Boda.Dave: THIS IS RIDICULOUS! BODA IS OUT HERE NOW!!!!Jeff: WHY IS HE WEARING THOSE RIDICULOUS LOOKING USA/APOLLO CREED TRUNKS?!?!Boda climbs the ladder and heads straight for Stanislav. Jeff: Boda must have a confidence rush after beating down that pipsqueak Mike Storm!!!The two big men start to go at it exchanging right and left hands. Then the four Canadians on top of the cell all pile on Stanislav and help to pound him down to his knees.Dave: It’s taking everything those five men can muster to get that Russian monster down!Nosferatu still has Walczak tied up, now being aided by Stryfe, and Kanton and USA Kid are taking it to Morozov and Ruslan. Then a disturbing noise starts to be heard throughout the arena. Dave: WHAT IS THAT NOISE?! I THINK IT’S THE CELL! IT’S GOING TO COLLAPSE!!Jeff: COMRADES!! GET OFF OF THERE!!The cell starts to sway back and forth and the top begins to sink in. The wrestlers on top have all stopped their assaults on each other and are looking around for an escape. It is too late however as the top of the cage suddenly drops out from underneath them and falls to the mat followed by all of the wrestlers that were on top.The Crowd: OOOOOOOOoooooooooo!!!!!!Dave: OH MY GOD!!!!!!!!!Jeff: NOOOOOOOOO!!! COMRADES!!Steven: BOOOOOOCA!!!!All of the men on top of the cell are strewn all around the ringside area, laying motionless. Dave: WE ARE OUT OF TIME!!!!Jeff: NOOOOOOOOOO!!!!!Dave: WE NEED SOME HELP OUT HERE!! GET THE DOCTORS OUT HERE!!Jeff: CHECK ON MY COMRADES!!!The camera shifts to the entryway as a paramedics begin streaming out of the back.Dave: ALL HELL HAS BROKEN LOOSE HERE TONIGHT!!! WE WILL TRY TO SORT OUT ALL OF THE RAMIFICATIONS OF WHAT WENT DOWN HERE TONIGHT THIS SUNDAY ON SABOTAGE!!! GOOD NIGHT!!!The camera focuses in on the dozen or so motionless bodies lying on top of the steel cage….as the Copyright 2001 Primetime Championship Wrestling Logo fades into the lower, central portion of the screen…..and the show fades to black……..
